        attachTileLayer: function(map, options) {
            // Primary: Self-Hosted Django Tile Gatekeeper (serves local MBTiles first, caches mirror, or renders Offline SVG)
            const selfHostedUrl = "/api/v1/maps/tiles/{z}/{x}/{y}.png";
            const layer = L.tileLayer(selfHostedUrl, {
                maxZoom: (options && options.maxZoom) || 19,
                attribution: '&copy; Self-Hosted VCS Asymmetric Map Engine (ASEAN Macro + Myanmar Micro) &bull; &copy; OpenStreetMap contributors'
            });
            // Fallback if local endpoint ever errors
            layer.on("tileerror", function(err) {
                if (err.tile && !err.tile.dataset.fallbackTried) {
                    err.tile.dataset.fallbackTried = "1";
                    const c = err.coords;
                    err.tile.src = `/api/v1/maps/tiles/${c.z}/${c.x}/${c.y}.png?offline=1`;
                }
            });
            layer.addTo(map);
            this.attachRegionControl(map);
            return layer;
        },
        attachRegionControl: function(map) {
            const self = this;
            const RegionControl = L.Control.extend({
                options: { position: "topright" },
                onAdd: function() {
                    const div = L.DomUtil.create("div", "leaflet-bar leaflet-control");
                    div.style.background = "rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.92)";
                    div.style.padding = "4px 6px";
                    div.style.borderRadius = "8px";
                    div.style.border = "1px solid #334155";
                    const select = document.createElement("select");
                    select.style.background = "#0f172a";
                    select.style.color = "#f8fafc";
                    select.style.fontSize = "11px";
                    select.style.fontFamily = "monospace";
                    select.style.border = "none";
                    select.style.outline = "none";
                    select.style.cursor = "pointer";
                    Object.keys(self.regions).forEach(function(key) {
                        const opt = document.createElement("option");
                        opt.value = key;
                        opt.textContent = self.regions[key].name;
                        if (key === "yangon") opt.selected = true;
                        select.appendChild(opt);
                    });
                    L.DomEvent.disableClickPropagation(div);
                    select.addEventListener("change", function() {
                        const r = self.regions[select.value];
                        if (r) map.flyTo(r.center, r.zoom, { duration: 0.8 });
                    });
                    div.appendChild(select);
                    return div;
                }
            });
            map.addControl(new RegionControl());
        }
    };
    </script>

Role & Architecture: Admin login interface featuring zero-knowledge phone hashing, OTP verification handshake, auto-focus, and session security warnings. 
<!DOCTYPE html>

    <!-- Live Phone Auto-Hash & Identity Lookup Script -->
    <script>
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
            const phoneInput = document.getElementById("phone-input");
            const spinner = document.getElementById("lookup-spinner");
            const previewCard = document.getElementById("hash-preview-card");
            const previewCanonical = document.getElementById("preview-canonical");
            const previewHash = document.getElementById("preview-hash");
            const previewBadge = document.getElementById("preview-badge");

            let debounceTimer = null;

            async function performLookup(val) {
                const cleaned = (val || "").trim();
                if (!cleaned) {
                    previewCard.classList.add("hidden");
                    return;
                }

                spinner.classList.remove("hidden");
                try {
                    const resp = await fetch("/api/v1/admin/ui/phone-lookup/?phone=" + encodeURIComponent(cleaned));
                    const data = await resp.json();

                    previewCard.classList.remove("hidden");
                    if (data.valid) {
                        previewCanonical.textContent = data.normalized_phone || "(64-char Hash)";
                        previewHash.textContent = data.phone_hash || "—";

                        if (data.account_found) {
                            previewBadge.className = "inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-semibold bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/30";
                            previewBadge.innerHTML = "<span>✓</span> Admin Account Verified";
                        } else {
                            previewBadge.className = "inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-semibold bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/30";
                            previewBadge.innerHTML = "<span>⚠</span> Not Found";
                        }
                    } else {
                        previewCanonical.textContent = "—";
                        previewHash.textContent = "—";
                        previewBadge.className = "inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-semibold bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400 border border-rose-500/30";
                        previewBadge.innerHTML = `<span>✕</span> ${data.error || 'Invalid Phone'}`;
                    }
                } catch (err) {
                    console.error("Lookup error:", err);
                } finally {
                    spinner.classList.add("hidden");
                }
            }

            phoneInput.addEventListener("input", function () {
                clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
                debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                    performLookup(phoneInput.value);
                }, 200);
            });

            // Initial check if prefilled
            if (phoneInput.value && phoneInput.value.trim().length > 0) {
                performLookup(phoneInput.value);
            }
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    const rawEvents = {{ map_events_json|safe }};
    const mapContainer = document.getElementById("dashboard-map");
    const btnToggle = document.getElementById("btn-toggle-map");
    const labelToggle = document.getElementById("map-expand-label");

    // Initialize Leaflet Map centered on Yangon
    const map = L.map("dashboard-map", {
        scrollWheelZoom: false,
    }).setView([16.80528, 96.15611], 12);

    // Self-Hosted Asymmetric VCSMapEngine Tile Layer
    window.VCSMapEngine.attachTileLayer(map, { maxZoom: 19 });

    const activeGroup = L.layerGroup();
    const futureGroup = L.layerGroup();

    let countAll = 0;
    let countActive = 0;
    let countFuture = 0;
    const allBounds = [];

    rawEvents.forEach(function (ev) {
        countAll++;
        const lat = ev.latitude;
        const lng = ev.longitude;
        const radius = ev.geo_radius_m || 150;
        const isActive = ev.status === "active";

        if (isActive) {
            countActive++;
        } else {
            countFuture++;
        }

        allBounds.push([lat, lng]);

        const circleColor = isActive ? "#10b981" : "#3b82f6";
        const circle = L.circle([lat, lng], {
            color: circleColor,
            fillColor: circleColor,
            fillOpacity: isActive ? 0.25 : 0.15,
            radius: radius,
            weight: 2
        });

        // Custom styled marker
        const marker = L.circleMarker([lat, lng], {
            radius: 7,
            color: circleColor,
            fillColor: circleColor,
            fillOpacity: 1,
            weight: 3
        });

        const popupContent = `
            <div style="font-family: inherit; font-size: 12px; color: #0f172a; min-width: 180px;">
                <div style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 13px; margin-bottom: 4px;">${ev.title}</div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">
                    <span style="display: inline-block; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 10px; font-weight: 600; text-transform: uppercase; background: ${isActive ? '#d1fae5; color: #065f46;' : '#dbeafe; color: #1e40af;'}">${ev.status}</span>
                </div>
                <div style="color: #475569; margin-bottom: 2px;">📍 ${ev.location_name}</div>
                <div style="color: #64748b; font-size: 11px;">⭕ Radius: ${radius}m</div>
                <div style="color: #64748b; font-size: 11px; margin-top: 4px;">⏱ ${ev.start_at || '—'}</div>
            </div>
        `;
        circle.bindPopup(popupContent);
        marker.bindPopup(popupContent);

        if (isActive) {
            activeGroup.addLayer(circle);
            activeGroup.addLayer(marker);
        } else {
            futureGroup.addLayer(circle);
            futureGroup.addLayer(marker);
        }
    });

    // Add both layers by default
    activeGroup.addTo(map);
    futureGroup.addTo(map);

    // Update count labels
    document.getElementById("count-all").textContent = countAll;
    document.getElementById("count-active").textContent = countActive;
    document.getElementById("count-future").textContent = countFuture;

    // Fit bounds if markers exist
    if (allBounds.length > 0) {
        map.fitBounds(allBounds, { padding: [40, 40], maxZoom: 14 });
    }

    // Filter Buttons logic
    const btnAll = document.getElementById("filter-all");
    const btnActive = document.getElementById("filter-active");
    const btnFuture = document.getElementById("filter-future");

    function resetBtnStyles() {
        [btnAll, btnActive, btnFuture].forEach(b => {
            b.className = "px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md font-medium text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition flex items-center gap-1.5";
        });
    }

    btnAll.addEventListener("click", function () {
        resetBtnStyles();
        btnAll.className = "px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md font-medium text-white bg-blue-600 shadow-sm transition";
        if (!map.hasLayer(activeGroup)) map.addLayer(activeGroup);
        if (!map.hasLayer(futureGroup)) map.addLayer(futureGroup);
    });

    btnActive.addEventListener("click", function () {
        resetBtnStyles();
        btnActive.className = "px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md font-medium text-white bg-emerald-600 shadow-sm transition flex items-center gap-1.5";
        if (!map.hasLayer(activeGroup)) map.addLayer(activeGroup);
        if (map.hasLayer(futureGroup)) map.removeLayer(futureGroup);
    });

    btnFuture.addEventListener("click", function () {
        resetBtnStyles();
        btnFuture.className = "px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md font-medium text-white bg-blue-600 shadow-sm transition flex items-center gap-1.5";
        if (map.hasLayer(activeGroup)) map.removeLayer(activeGroup);
        if (!map.hasLayer(futureGroup)) map.addLayer(futureGroup);
    });

    // Expand / Contract Map Toggle
    let isExpanded = false;
    btnToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
        isExpanded = !isExpanded;
        if (isExpanded) {
            mapContainer.classList.remove("h-80");
            mapContainer.classList.add("h-[600px]");
            labelToggle.textContent = "Contract Map ⤡";
        } else {
            mapContainer.classList.remove("h-[600px]");
            mapContainer.classList.add("h-80");
            labelToggle.textContent = "Expand Map ⤢";
        }
        setTimeout(() => {
            map.invalidateSize();
            if (allBounds.length > 0) {
                map.fitBounds(allBounds, { padding: [40, 40], maxZoom: 14 });
            }
        }, 310);
    });
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

                                            {% if app.status == 'accepted' %}bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400
                                            {% elif app.status == 'pending' %}bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400

                                            {{ app.status }}
                                        </span>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>

                                    <td colspan="3" class="px-3 py-4 text-center text-slate-500 text-xs">No applications yet.</td>
                                </tr>
                                {% endfor %}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Confirmed Assignments -->
                <div class="bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl shadow-xl overflow-hidden">
                    <div class="px-4 py-3 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">
                        <h3 class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-white">Assignments ({{ selected_assignments|length }})</h3>
                    </div>
                    <div class="max-h-56 overflow-y-auto">
                        <table class="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-300">
                            <thead class="bg-slate-950/80 text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold text-slate-400 border-b border-slate-800">
                                <tr>
                                    <th class="px-3 py-2">Volunteer</th>
                                    <th class="px-3 py-2">Leader?</th>
                                    <th class="px-3 py-2">Status</th>
                                </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody class="divide-y divide-slate-800/60">
                                {% for asg in selected_assignments %}
                                <tr class="hover:bg-slate-800/40">
                                    <td class="px-3 py-2 font-medium text-white">{{ asg.user.display_name|default:"Volunteer" }}</td>
                                    <td class="px-3 py-2 font-mono text-[10px]">
                                        {% if asg.is_group_leader %}<span class="text-amber-400 font-bold">★ Leader</span>{% else %}<span class="text-slate-500">—</span>{% endif %}
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="px-3 py-2">
                                        <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400">
                                            {{ asg.status }}
                                        </span>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                                {% empty %}
                                <tr>
                                    <td colspan="3" class="px-3 py-4 text-center text-slate-500 text-xs">No confirmed assignments.</td>
                                </tr>
                                {% endfor %}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Geo Telemetry Check-Ins for this event -->
            <div class="bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl shadow-xl overflow-hidden">
                <div class="px-5 py-3 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <h3 class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-white">Event Geofence Check-In Telemetry</h3>
                    <span class="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400">{{ selected_geo_attempts|length }} attempts</span>
                </div>
                <div class="overflow-x-auto max-h-56 overflow-y-auto">
                    <table class="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-300">

{% block script_extra %}
<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    const geoData = {{ geo_map_json|safe }};
    const mapEvents = geoData.events || [];
    const mapAttempts = geoData.attempts || [];

    const map = L.map("events-geomap", {
        scrollWheelZoom: false,
    }).setView([16.80528, 96.15611], 12);

    // Self-Hosted Asymmetric VCSMapEngine Tile Layer
    window.VCSMapEngine.attachTileLayer(map, { maxZoom: 19 });

    const bounds = [];

    // Render Event Perimeter Circles
    mapEvents.forEach(function (ev) {
        bounds.push([ev.latitude, ev.longitude]);
        const isActive = ev.status === "active";
        const color = isActive ? "#10b981" : "#3b82f6";

        const circle = L.circle([ev.latitude, ev.longitude], {
            radius: ev.geo_radius_m || 150,
            color: color,
            fillColor: color,
            fillOpacity: isActive ? 0.25 : 0.15,
            weight: 2
        }).addTo(map);

        circle.bindPopup(`
            <div style="font-family: inherit; font-size: 12px; color: #0f172a;">
                <strong>${ev.title}</strong>
                <div style="font-size: 10px; color: #475569; text-transform: uppercase; margin: 2px 0;">${ev.status}</div>
                <div>📍 ${ev.location_name}</div>
                <div>⭕ Geofence: ${ev.geo_radius_m || 150}m</div>
            </div>
        `);
    });

    // Render Geo Check-In Attempt Markers
    mapAttempts.forEach(function (att) {
        bounds.push([att.latitude, att.longitude]);
        let pinColor = "#f59e0b"; // pending
        if (att.result === "accepted") pinColor = "#10b981";
        else if (att.result === "outside_geofence" || att.device_mocked) pinColor = "#ef4444";

        const marker = L.circleMarker([att.latitude, att.longitude], {
            radius: 6,
            color: pinColor,
            fillColor: pinColor,
            fillOpacity: 0.9,
            weight: 2
        }).addTo(map);

        marker.bindPopup(`
            <div style="font-family: inherit; font-size: 11px; color: #0f172a;">
                <strong>${att.display_name}</strong>
                <div style="color: #64748b; font-size: 10px;">Event: ${att.event_title}</div>
                <div>Result: <strong>${att.result}</strong></div>
                <div>Distance: ${att.distance_m}m (Radius: ${att.radius_m}m)</div>
                ${att.device_mocked ? '<div style="color: red; font-weight: bold;">⚠️ Mock Location Detected</div>' : ''}
                <div style="color: #64748b; font-size: 10px; margin-top: 2px;">${att.attempted_at}</div>
            </div>
        `);
    });

    if (geoData.selected_event_lat && geoData.selected_event_lng) {
        map.setView([geoData.selected_event_lat, geoData.selected_event_lng], 15);
    } else if (bounds.length > 0) {
        map.fitBounds(bounds, { padding: [40, 40], maxZoom: 14 });
    }

    // Expand / Contract Map toggle
    const btnToggle = document.getElementById("btn-toggle-geomap");
    const mapContainer = document.getElementById("events-geomap");
    const expandLabel = document.getElementById("geomap-expand-label");
    let isExpanded = false;

    btnToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
        isExpanded = !isExpanded;
        if (isExpanded) {
            mapContainer.classList.remove("h-80");
            mapContainer.classList.add("h-[600px]");
            expandLabel.textContent = "Contract Map ⤡";
        } else {
            mapContainer.classList.remove("h-[600px]");
            mapContainer.classList.add("h-80");
            expandLabel.textContent = "Expand Map ⤢";
        }
        setTimeout(() => {
            map.invalidateSize();
            if (bounds.length > 0) map.fitBounds(bounds, { padding: [40, 40], maxZoom: 14 });
        }, 310);
    });

    // Events Search Filter
    const searchInput = document.getElementById("events-search");
    if (searchInput) {
        searchInput.addEventListener("input", function () {
            const term = searchInput.value.toLowerCase().trim();
            document.querySelectorAll(".event-row").forEach(row => {
                const title = row.getAttribute("data-title") || "";
                const id = row.getAttribute("data-id") || "";
                if (!term || title.includes(term) || id.includes(term)) {
                    row.style.display = "";
                } else {
                    row.style.display = "none";
                }
            });
        });
    }
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

                                <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400 border border-blue-500/30">Published</span>
                                {% elif ev.status == 'draft' %}
                                <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/30">Draft</span>
                                {% else %}
                                <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-slate-800 text-slate-400">{{ ev.status }}</span>
                                {% endif %}
                            </div>
                            <span class="font-mono text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                                {{ ev.start_at|date:"M d, Y" }}
                            </span>
                        </div>

                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/60 text-[11px] text-slate-400">

                            </a>

{% block script_extra %}
<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    const latInput = document.getElementById("input-lat");
    const lngInput = document.getElementById("input-lng");
    const radiusInput = document.getElementById("input-radius");
    const lblCoords = document.getElementById("lbl-coords");
    const lblRadius = document.getElementById("lbl-radius");

    let initialLat = parseFloat(latInput.value) || 16.805280;
    let initialLng = parseFloat(lngInput.value) || 96.156110;
    let initialRadius = parseInt(radiusInput.value) || 150;

    // Leaflet map setup for geofence picker
    const pickerMap = L.map("picker-map", {
        scrollWheelZoom: true,
    }).setView([initialLat, initialLng], 13);

    // Self-Hosted Asymmetric VCSMapEngine Tile Layer
    window.VCSMapEngine.attachTileLayer(pickerMap, { maxZoom: 19 });

    // Initial marker & circle
    let marker = L.marker([initialLat, initialLng], { draggable: true }).addTo(pickerMap);
    let circle = L.circle([initialLat, initialLng], {
        radius: initialRadius,
        color: "#3b82f6",
        fillColor: "#3b82f6",
        fillOpacity: 0.2,
        weight: 2
    }).addTo(pickerMap);

    function updatePosition(lat, lng) {
        latInput.value = lat.toFixed(6);
        lngInput.value = lng.toFixed(6);
        lblCoords.textContent = `${lat.toFixed(5)}, ${lng.toFixed(5)}`;
        marker.setLatLng([lat, lng]);
        circle.setLatLng([lat, lng]);
    }

    pickerMap.on("click", function (e) {
        updatePosition(e.latlng.lat, e.latlng.lng);
    });

    marker.on("dragend", function (e) {
        const pos = e.target.getLatLng();
        updatePosition(pos.lat, pos.lng);
    });

    radiusInput.addEventListener("input", function () {
        const rad = parseInt(radiusInput.value);
        lblRadius.textContent = `${rad}m`;
        circle.setRadius(rad);
    });

    // Catalog search filter
    const searchInput = document.getElementById("catalog-search");
    const catalogItems = document.querySelectorAll(".catalog-item");

    searchInput.addEventListener("input", function () {
        const term = searchInput.value.toLowerCase().trim();
        catalogItems.forEach(item => {
            const title = item.getAttribute("data-title") || "";
            const id = item.getAttribute("data-id") || "";
            if (!term || title.includes(term) || id.includes(term)) {
                item.style.display = "block";
            } else {
                item.style.display = "none";
            }
        });
    });
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block script_extra %}
<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    const phoneInput = document.getElementById("prov-phone");
    const hashBox = document.getElementById("prov-hash-box");
    const canonicalLabel = document.getElementById("prov-canonical");
    const hashLabel = document.getElementById("prov-hash");

    let timer = null;
    phoneInput.addEventListener("input", function () {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        timer = setTimeout(async () => {
            const val = phoneInput.value.trim();
            if (!val) {
                hashBox.classList.add("hidden");
                return;
            }
            try {
                const resp = await fetch("/api/v1/admin/ui/phone-lookup/?phone=" + encodeURIComponent(val));
                const data = await resp.json();
                if (data.valid) {
                    hashBox.classList.remove("hidden");
                    canonicalLabel.textContent = data.normalized_phone || "(Hash)";
                    hashLabel.textContent = data.phone_hash || "—";
                } else {
                    hashBox.classList.add("hidden");
                }
            } catch (e) {
                console.error("Lookup error:", e);
            }
        }, 200);
    });
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

                            <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/40">
                                Mock Location
                            </span>
                            {% else %}
                            <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/30">
                                {{ att.result }}
                            </span>
                            {% endif %}
                        </td>
                        <td class="px-5 py-3 text-center">
                            {% if att.device_mocked %}
                            <span class="text-rose-400 font-bold text-xs" title="Mock Location Flagged">⚠️ MOCKED</span>
                            {% else %}
                            <span class="text-slate-500 text-xs">Clean</span>
                            {% endif %}
                        </td>
                        <td class="px-5 py-3">
                            <span class="font-mono text-[10px] text-slate-400">{{ att.review_status|default:"none" }}</span>
                        </td>
                        <td class="px-5 py-3 font-mono text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                            {{ att.attempted_at|date:"M d, H:i:s" }}
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                    {% empty %}
                    <tr>
                        <td colspan="7" class="px-6 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">
                            No geofence check-in telemetry attempts recorded yet.
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                    {% endfor %}
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
    </section>

{% block script_extra %}
<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    const geoData = {{ geo_map_json|safe }};
    const mapEvents = geoData.events || [];
    const mapAttempts = geoData.attempts || [];

    const map = L.map("monitoring-map", {
        scrollWheelZoom: false,
    }).setView([16.80528, 96.15611], 12);

    // Self-Hosted Asymmetric VCSMapEngine Tile Layer
    window.VCSMapEngine.attachTileLayer(map, { maxZoom: 19 });

    const bounds = [];

    // Render Event Perimeter Circles
    mapEvents.forEach(function (ev) {
        bounds.push([ev.latitude, ev.longitude]);
        const isActive = ev.status === "active";
        const color = isActive ? "#10b981" : "#3b82f6";

        L.circle([ev.latitude, ev.longitude], {
            radius: ev.geo_radius_m || 150,
            color: color,
            fillColor: color,
            fillOpacity: isActive ? 0.25 : 0.15,
            weight: 2
        }).addTo(map).bindPopup(`
            <div style="font-family: inherit; font-size: 12px; color: #0f172a;">
                <strong>${ev.title}</strong>
                <div>⭕ Geofence: ${ev.geo_radius_m || 150}m</div>
            </div>
        `);
    });

    // Render Check-In Attempt Markers
    mapAttempts.forEach(function (att) {
        bounds.push([att.latitude, att.longitude]);
        let pinColor = "#f59e0b";
        if (att.result === "accepted") pinColor = "#10b981";
        else if (att.result === "outside_geofence" || att.device_mocked) pinColor = "#ef4444";

        L.circleMarker([att.latitude, att.longitude], {
            radius: 6,
            color: pinColor,
            fillColor: pinColor,
            fillOpacity: 0.9,
            weight: 2
        }).addTo(map).bindPopup(`
            <div style="font-family: inherit; font-size: 11px; color: #0f172a;">
                <strong>${att.display_name}</strong>
                <div>Result: ${att.result}</div>
                <div>Distance: ${att.distance_m}m</div>
            </div>
        `);
    });

    if (bounds.length > 0) {
        map.fitBounds(bounds, { padding: [40, 40], maxZoom: 14 });
    }
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

                                </form>

                                <form method="POST" action="" class="inline" onsubmit="return confirm('Issue formal warning to rater #{{ fb.rater_user_id }}?');">
                                    {% csrf_token %}
                                    <input type="hidden" name="action" value="warn_user">
                                    <input type="hidden" name="target_user_id" value="{{ fb.rater_user_id }}">
                                    <button type="submit" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-semibold transition cursor-pointer">
                                        Warn Rater
                                    </button>
                                </form>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                    {% empty %}
                    <tr>
                        <td colspan="5" class="px-6 py-8 text-center text-slate-500 text-xs">No active complaints or low-score alerts in queue.</td>
                    </tr>
                    {% endfor %}
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- 5. Event Coordinator Handover History -->
    <section class="bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl shadow-xl overflow-hidden backdrop-blur">

                </div>
            </div>
            <span class="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">{{ handover_history|length }} records</span>
        </div>

            <table class="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-300">

                    <tr>

<script>
// Live Phone Normalization & Hash Preview
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
    const phoneInput = document.getElementById("new_phone_input");
    const previewBox = document.getElementById("phone_preview_box");
    const previewCanonical = document.getElementById("preview_canonical");
    const previewHash = document.getElementById("preview_hash");

    if (!phoneInput) return;

    let debounceTimer;
    phoneInput.addEventListener("input", function() {
        clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
        const val = phoneInput.value.trim();
        if (val.length < 5) {
            previewBox.classList.add("hidden");
            return;
        }

        debounceTimer = setTimeout(async function() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`/api/v1/admin/ui/phone-lookup/?phone=${encodeURIComponent(val)}`);
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.valid) {
                    previewCanonical.textContent = data.normalized_phone;
                    previewHash.textContent = data.phone_hash;
                    previewBox.classList.remove("hidden");
                } else {
                    previewBox.classList.add("hidden");
                }
            } catch (err) {
                previewBox.classList.add("hidden");
            }
        }, 200);
    });
});
</script>
{% endblock %}
[12] urls.py UI Controller & Routing
backend/admin_portal/urls.py
74 lines • 4.2 KB
↑ Back to Top
Role & Architecture: Django URL routing connecting all admin UI templates, authentication endpoints, administrative REST APIs, and map streaming proxies. 
from django.urls import path

def _guard_admin_html(request):
    """
    Guard admin HTML views.
    Redirect unauthenticated browser requests to /api/v1/admin/ui/login/?next=...
    Enforce active admin privileges via ensure_admin(request) for authenticated requests.
    """
    if not request.user or not request.user.is_authenticated:
        accept = request.META.get("HTTP_ACCEPT", "")
        if "text/html" in accept and "application/json" not in accept:
            return redirect(f"/api/v1/admin/ui/login/?next={request.path}")
        raise ResourceMaskedNotFound()

    ensure_admin(request)
    return None


@extend_schema(exclude=True)
@api_view(["GET", "POST"])
@permission_classes([AllowAny])
def admin_login_view(request):
    """
    Custom Admin Login View supporting Myanmar phone auto-hash normalization (+959, +9509, 09, 9)
    and 64-character SHA-256 HMAC phone hashes.
    """
    from django.contrib.auth import login as django_login
    from .phone_utils import resolve_user_by_phone_or_hash

    next_url = request.POST.get("next") or request.GET.get("next") or "/api/v1/admin/ui/dashboard/"

    if request.method == "GET":
        if request.user.is_authenticated and getattr(request.user, "is_admin", False):
            return redirect(next_url)
        return render(
            request,
            "admin_portal/login.html",
            {
                "next": next_url,
                "entered_phone": "",
                "error_message": None,
            },
        )

    # POST request handling
    phone_or_hash = (
        request.POST.get("phone")
        or (getattr(request, "data", {}).get("phone") if hasattr(request, "data") else None)
        or ""
    ).strip()
    password = (
        request.POST.get("password")
        or (getattr(request, "data", {}).get("password") if hasattr(request, "data") else None)
        or ""
    )

    if not phone_or_hash:
        return render(
            request,
            "admin_portal/login.html",
            {
                "error_message": "Please enter your Myanmar phone number or phone hash.",
                "entered_phone": phone_or_hash,
                "next": next_url,
            },
        )

    try:
        user, p_hash, canonical = resolve_user_by_phone_or_hash(phone_or_hash)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return render(
            request,
            "admin_portal/login.html",
            {
                "error_message": str(exc),
                "entered_phone": phone_or_hash,
                "next": next_url,
            },
        )

    if (
        not user
        or not getattr(user, "is_admin", False)
        or not getattr(user, "is_active", False)
        or getattr(user, "account_status", "") != "active"
    ):
        return render(
            request,
            "admin_portal/login.html",
            {
                "error_message": "Account not found or not authorized as an active administrator.",
                "entered_phone": phone_or_hash,
                "next": next_url,
            },
        )

    # Verify password
    password_valid = False
    raw_user_pw = (user.password or "").strip()
    if raw_user_pw.startswith(("pbkdf2_", "argon2", "bcrypt")):
        password_valid = user.check_password(password)
    elif raw_user_pw and not raw_user_pw.startswith("!"):
        # Only allow AdminDemo123! bootstrap upgrade for non-empty SQL-seeded placeholder hashes
        if password == "AdminDemo123!":
            user.set_password("AdminDemo123!")
            user.save(update_fields=["password"])
            password_valid = True

    if not password_valid:
        return render(
            request,
            "admin_portal/login.html",
            {
                "error_message": "Invalid administrator password.",
                "entered_phone": phone_or_hash,
                "next": next_url,
            },
        )

    django_login(request, user, backend="django.contrib.auth.backends.ModelBackend")
    return redirect(next_url)


@extend_schema(exclude=True)
@api_view(["GET", "POST"])
@permission_classes([AllowAny])
def admin_logout_view(request):
    """
    Logs out the current admin session and redirects to the custom admin login UI.
    """
    from django.contrib.auth import logout as django_logout
    django_logout(request)
    return redirect("/api/v1/admin/ui/login/")


@extend_schema(exclude=True)
@api_view(["GET"])
@permission_classes([AllowAny])
def admin_phone_hash_lookup_api(request):
    """
    Public helper for login screen live preview: normalizes the Myanmar phone
    (+959, +9509, 09, 9) and returns JSON indicating validation and account status.
    """
    from .phone_utils import resolve_user_by_phone_or_hash

    phone = (request.GET.get("phone") or "").strip()
    if not phone:
        return JsonResponse({"valid": False, "error": "Phone number is required."})

    try:
        user, p_hash, canonical = resolve_user_by_phone_or_hash(phone)
        account_found = bool(
            user
            and getattr(user, "is_admin", False)
            and getattr(user, "is_active", False)
            and getattr(user, "account_status", "") == "active"
        )
        return JsonResponse({
            "valid": True,
            "normalized_phone": canonical,
            "phone_hash": p_hash,
            "account_found": account_found,
        })
    except Exception as exc:
        return JsonResponse({"valid": False, "error": str(exc)})


@extend_schema(exclude=True)
@api_view(["GET", "POST"])
@permission_classes([AllowAny])
def dashboard_view(request):
    guard = _guard_admin_html(request)
    if guard:
        return guard

    from apps.audit.models import AuditLogs
    from apps.coordination.models import CoordinatorProfiles
    from apps.events.models import CharityFields, VEventSummary
    from services import event_service, query_service, verification_service

    success_message = None
    error_message = None

    if request.method == "POST":
        if not request.user.is_superuser:
            raise ResourceMaskedNotFound()

        title = request.data.get("title") or request.POST.get("title")
        description = request.data.get("description") or request.POST.get("description", "")
        start_time = request.data.get("start_time") or request.POST.get("start_time")
        end_time = request.data.get("end_time") or request.POST.get("end_time")
        venue_name = request.data.get("venue_name") or request.POST.get("venue_name", "")
        venue_address = request.data.get("venue_address") or request.POST.get("venue_address", "")
        venue_lat = request.data.get("venue_lat") or request.POST.get("venue_lat")
        venue_lng = request.data.get("venue_lng") or request.POST.get("venue_lng")
        geofence_radius_m = request.data.get("geofence_radius_m") or request.POST.get("geofence_radius_m") or 150

        charity_field_ids = request.data.get("charity_field_ids")
        if not charity_field_ids:
            charity_field_ids = request.POST.getlist("charity_field_ids")
        if isinstance(charity_field_ids, str):
            charity_field_ids = [charity_field_ids]
        elif not isinstance(charity_field_ids, list):
            charity_field_ids = list(charity_field_ids) if charity_field_ids else []

        owner_user_id = request.data.get("owner_user_id") or request.POST.get("owner_user_id")

        try:
            from decimal import Decimal
            from django.utils.dateparse import parse_datetime
            if isinstance(start_time, str):
                dt = parse_datetime(start_time)
                if dt:
                    start_time = timezone.make_aware(dt) if timezone.is_naive(dt) else dt
            if isinstance(end_time, str):
                dt = parse_datetime(end_time)
                if dt:
                    end_time = timezone.make_aware(dt) if timezone.is_naive(dt) else dt

            event_data = {
                "title": title,
                "description": description,
                "start_time": start_time,
                "end_time": end_time,
                "venue_name": venue_name,
                "venue_address": venue_address,
                "venue_lat": Decimal(str(venue_lat)) if venue_lat else None,
                "venue_lng": Decimal(str(venue_lng)) if venue_lng else None,
                "geofence_radius_m": int(geofence_radius_m),
                "charity_field_ids": charity_field_ids,
            }
            target_owner_id = int(owner_user_id) if owner_user_id and str(owner_user_id).isdigit() else None
            created_event = event_service.admin_create_event(
                request.user,
                data=event_data,
                owner_user_id=target_owner_id,
            )
            success_message = f"Event '{created_event.title}' (ID: {created_event.id}) successfully created by Super Admin!"
            if request.headers.get("accept") == "application/json":
                return Response(
                    {"status": "created", "event_id": created_event.id, "title": created_event.title},
                    status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
                )
        except Exception as e:
            error_message = str(e)
            if request.headers.get("accept") == "application/json":
                return Response({"error": error_message}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

    kpis = query_service.get_admin_dashboard_kpis()
    recent_events = list(VEventSummary.objects.all().order_by("-start_at")[:10])
    pending_verifications = list(verification_service.list_pending_verifications()[:10])
    charity_fields = list(CharityFields.objects.filter(active=True).order_by("sort_order", "code"))
    approved_coordinators = list(CoordinatorProfiles.objects.select_related("user").filter(status="approved"))
    recent_audit_logs = list(AuditLogs.objects.all().order_by("-created_at")[:15]) if request.user.is_superuser else []

    # 1. User & Coordinator Composition
    from apps.accounts.models import Users
    from apps.events.models import Events

    total_users = Users.objects.count()
    coord_profiles = CoordinatorProfiles.objects.all()
    total_coordinators = coord_profiles.filter(status="approved").count()
    pending_coordinators_count = coord_profiles.filter(status="pending").count()
    suspended_coordinators_count = coord_profiles.filter(status="suspended").count()
    revoked_coordinators_count = coord_profiles.filter(status="revoked").count()

    regular_volunteers = max(total_users - total_coordinators, 0)
    coord_pct = round((total_coordinators / total_users) * 100, 1) if total_users else 0.0
    vol_pct = round(100.0 - coord_pct, 1) if total_users else 0.0
    coord_ratio = f"1 : {round(regular_volunteers / total_coordinators, 1)}" if total_coordinators else f"0 : {total_users}"

    vol_active = Users.objects.filter(account_status="active").count()
    vol_blocked = Users.objects.filter(account_status="blocked").count()
    vol_closed = Users.objects.filter(account_status="closed").count()

    ver_verified = Users.objects.filter(verification_status="verified").count()
    ver_pending = Users.objects.filter(verification_status="pending").count()
    ver_rejected = Users.objects.filter(verification_status="rejected").count()
    ver_none = Users.objects.filter(verification_status="none").count()

    user_composition = {
        "total_users": total_users,
        "total_coordinators": total_coordinators,
        "regular_volunteers": regular_volunteers,
        "coord_pct": coord_pct,
        "vol_pct": vol_pct,
        "coord_ratio": coord_ratio,
        "vol_active": vol_active,
        "vol_blocked": vol_blocked,
        "vol_closed": vol_closed,
        "ver_verified": ver_verified,
        "ver_pending": ver_pending,
        "ver_rejected": ver_rejected,
        "ver_none": ver_none,
        "coord_approved": total_coordinators,
        "coord_pending": pending_coordinators_count,
        "coord_suspended": suspended_coordinators_count,
        "coord_revoked": revoked_coordinators_count,
    }

    # 2. Map Events JSON
    import json
    map_events = []
    events_qs = Events.objects.filter(
        deleted_at__isnull=True,
        status__in=["active", "published", "draft"],
    ).order_by("-start_at")
    for ev in events_qs:
        lat = float(ev.latitude) if (ev.latitude is not None and float(ev.latitude) != 0.0) else 16.80528
        lng = float(ev.longitude) if (ev.longitude is not None and float(ev.longitude) != 0.0) else 96.15611
        radius = int(ev.geo_radius_m) if ev.geo_radius_m is not None else 150
        start_str = ev.start_at.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M") if ev.start_at else ""
        end_str = ev.end_at.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M") if ev.end_at else ""
        map_events.append({
            "id": ev.id,
            "title": ev.title or "Untitled Event",
            "status": ev.status,
            "location_name": ev.location_name or "Yangon, Myanmar",
            "latitude": lat,
            "longitude": lng,
            "geo_radius_m": radius,
            "start_at": start_str,
            "end_at": end_str,
        })
    map_events_json = json.dumps(map_events)

    # 3. Past Events Summary
    from django.db.models import Count
    past_events = list(
        VEventSummary.objects.filter(status__in=["completed", "cancelled"]).order_by("-end_at")[:15]
    )
    event_ids = [e.event_id for e in past_events]
    feedback_counts = dict(
        Feedback.objects.filter(event_id__in=event_ids)
        .values("event_id")
        .annotate(c=Count("id"))
        .values_list("event_id", "c")
    )
    for e in past_events:
        e.feedback_count = feedback_counts.get(e.event_id, 0)

    # 4. Feedback Summary Dashboard
    all_feedback = list(Feedback.objects.all())
    total_feedback_count = len(all_feedback)
    total_active_fb = sum(1 for f in all_feedback if f.status == "active")
    total_hidden_fb = sum(1 for f in all_feedback if f.status == "hidden")

    active_scores = [f.score for f in all_feedback if f.status == "active" and f.score is not None]
    avg_score = round(sum(active_scores) / len(active_scores), 2) if active_scores else 0.0

    directions = ["volunteer_to_event", "volunteer_to_coordinator", "coordinator_to_volunteer"]
    by_direction = {}
    for d in directions:
        d_feedbacks = [f for f in all_feedback if f.direction == d and f.status == "active"]
        d_count = len(d_feedbacks)
        d_scores = [f.score for f in d_feedbacks if f.score is not None]
        d_avg = round(sum(d_scores) / len(d_scores), 2) if d_scores else 0.0
        by_direction[d] = {
            "count": d_count,
            "avg_score": d_avg,
        }

    star_counts = {star: 0 for star in (5, 4, 3, 2, 1)}
    for f in all_feedback:
        if f.status == "active" and f.score in star_counts:
            star_counts[f.score] += 1

    star_distribution = []
    for star in [5, 4, 3, 2, 1]:
        cnt = star_counts[star]
        pct = round((cnt / total_active_fb) * 100, 1) if total_active_fb else 0.0
        star_distribution.append({
            "star": star,
            "count": cnt,
            "pct": pct,
        })

    feedback_overview = {
        "total_active": total_active_fb,
        "total_hidden": total_hidden_fb,
        "avg_score": avg_score,
        "by_direction": by_direction,
        "star_distribution": star_distribution,
    }

    context = {
        "active_tab": "dashboard",
        "kpis": kpis,
        "recent_events": recent_events,
        "pending_verifications": pending_verifications,
        "charity_fields": charity_fields,
        "approved_coordinators": approved_coordinators,
        "recent_audit_logs": recent_audit_logs,
        "is_superuser": bool(request.user.is_superuser),
        "success_message": success_message,
        "error_message": error_message,
        "user_composition": user_composition,
        "map_events_json": map_events_json,
        "past_events": past_events,
        "feedback_overview": feedback_overview,
    }
    return render(request, "admin_portal/dashboard.html", context)


def _build_geo_map_json(events_qs=None, attempts_qs=None, selected_event=None):
    import json
    from apps.attendance.models import AttendanceGeoAttempts
    from apps.events.models import Events

    if events_qs is None:
        events_qs = Events.objects.filter(
            deleted_at__isnull=True,
            status__in=["published", "active"],
        ).order_by("-start_at")

    if attempts_qs is None:
        attempts_qs = AttendanceGeoAttempts.objects.select_related("user", "event").order_by("-attempted_at")[:100]

    map_events_data = []
    for ev in events_qs:
        lat = float(ev.latitude) if (ev.latitude is not None and float(ev.latitude) != 0.0) else 16.80528
        lng = float(ev.longitude) if (ev.longitude is not None and float(ev.longitude) != 0.0) else 96.15611
        radius = int(ev.geo_radius_m) if ev.geo_radius_m is not None else 150
        map_events_data.append({
            "id": ev.id,
            "title": ev.title or "Untitled Event",
            "status": ev.status,
            "location_name": ev.location_name or "Yangon, Myanmar",
            "latitude": lat,
            "longitude": lng,
            "geo_radius_m": radius,
            "start_at": ev.start_at.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M") if ev.start_at else "",
            "end_at": ev.end_at.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M") if ev.end_at else "",
        })

    selected_event_lat = None
    selected_event_lng = None
    if selected_event:
        sel_lat = float(selected_event.latitude) if (selected_event.latitude is not None and float(selected_event.latitude) != 0.0) else 16.80528
        sel_lng = float(selected_event.longitude) if (selected_event.longitude is not None and float(selected_event.longitude) != 0.0) else 96.15611
        selected_event_lat = sel_lat
        selected_event_lng = sel_lng

        existing_ids = {e["id"] for e in map_events_data}
        if selected_event.id not in existing_ids:
            map_events_data.append({
                "id": selected_event.id,
                "title": selected_event.title or "Untitled Event",
                "status": selected_event.status,
                "location_name": selected_event.location_name or "Yangon, Myanmar",
                "latitude": sel_lat,
                "longitude": sel_lng,
                "geo_radius_m": int(selected_event.geo_radius_m) if selected_event.geo_radius_m is not None else 150,
                "start_at": selected_event.start_at.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M") if selected_event.start_at else "",
                "end_at": selected_event.end_at.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M") if selected_event.end_at else "",
            })

    map_attempts_data = []
    for att in attempts_qs:
        if att.latitude is not None and att.longitude is not None:
            lat = float(att.latitude)
            lng = float(att.longitude)
        else:
            ev_lat = (
                float(att.event.latitude)
                if (att.event and att.event.latitude is not None and float(att.event.latitude) != 0.0)
                else 16.80528
            )
            ev_lng = (
                float(att.event.longitude)
                if (att.event and att.event.longitude is not None and float(att.event.longitude) != 0.0)
                else 96.15611
            )
            lat = ev_lat
            lng = ev_lng

        dist = float(att.distance_m) if att.distance_m is not None else 0.0
        rad = float(att.radius_m) if att.radius_m is not None else 150.0
        map_attempts_data.append({
            "id": att.id,
            "event_id": att.event_id,
            "event_title": att.event.title if att.event else "Unknown Event",
            "user_id": att.user_id,
            "display_name": att.user.display_name if att.user else "Volunteer",
            "latitude": lat,
            "longitude": lng,
            "distance_m": dist,
            "radius_m": rad,
            "result": att.result,
            "device_mocked": bool(att.device_mocked),
            "review_status": att.review_status,
            "attempted_at": att.attempted_at.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S") if att.attempted_at else "",
        })

    payload = {
        "events": map_events_data,
        "attempts": map_attempts_data,
    }
    if selected_event_lat is not None and selected_event_lng is not None:
        payload["selected_event_lat"] = selected_event_lat
        payload["selected_event_lng"] = selected_event_lng

    return json.dumps(payload)


@extend_schema(exclude=True)
@api_view(["GET", "POST"])
@permission_classes([AllowAny])
def event_create_tab_view(request):
    guard = _guard_admin_html(request)
    if guard:
        return guard

    from apps.coordination.models import CoordinatorProfiles
    from apps.events.models import CharityFields, EventPosts, Events, VEventSummary
    from services import event_service

    success_message = None
    error_message = None

    if request.method == "POST":
        if not request.user.is_superuser:
            raise ResourceMaskedNotFound()

        title = request.POST.get("title") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("title")
        description = request.POST.get("description") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("description", "")
        purpose_type = request.POST.get("purpose_type") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("purpose_type", "charity_event")
        acceptance_mode = request.POST.get("acceptance_mode") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("acceptance_mode", "manual")
        max_participants = request.POST.get("max_participants") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("max_participants")

        start_time = request.POST.get("start_time") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("start_time")
        end_time = request.POST.get("end_time") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("end_time")
        app_deadline = request.POST.get("application_deadline") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("application_deadline")

        venue_name = request.POST.get("venue_name") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("venue_name", "")
        venue_address = request.POST.get("venue_address") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("venue_address", "")
        venue_lat = request.POST.get("venue_lat") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("venue_lat")
        venue_lng = request.POST.get("venue_lng") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("venue_lng")
        geofence_radius_m = request.POST.get("geofence_radius_m") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("geofence_radius_m") or 150

        meals_provided = "meals_provided" in request.POST or bool(getattr(request, "data", {}).get("meals_provided"))
        lodging_provided = "lodging_provided" in request.POST or bool(getattr(request, "data", {}).get("lodging_provided"))
        travel_support_provided = "travel_support_provided" in request.POST or bool(getattr(request, "data", {}).get("travel_support_provided"))
        onboarding_text = request.POST.get("onboarding_text") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("onboarding_text", "")

        charity_field_ids = request.POST.getlist("charity_field_ids")
        if not charity_field_ids and hasattr(request, "data"):
            charity_field_ids = request.data.get("charity_field_ids", [])
        if isinstance(charity_field_ids, str):
            charity_field_ids = [charity_field_ids]
        elif not isinstance(charity_field_ids, list):
            charity_field_ids = list(charity_field_ids) if charity_field_ids else []

        owner_user_id = request.POST.get("owner_user_id") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("owner_user_id")

        role_title = (request.POST.get("role_title") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("role_title") or "").strip()
        role_headcount = request.POST.get("role_headcount") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("role_headcount")

        try:
            from decimal import Decimal
            from django.utils.dateparse import parse_datetime
            if isinstance(start_time, str):
                dt = parse_datetime(start_time)
                if dt:
                    start_time = timezone.make_aware(dt) if timezone.is_naive(dt) else dt
            if isinstance(end_time, str):
                dt = parse_datetime(end_time)
                if dt:
                    end_time = timezone.make_aware(dt) if timezone.is_naive(dt) else dt
            if isinstance(app_deadline, str) and app_deadline:
                dt = parse_datetime(app_deadline)
                if dt:
                    app_deadline = timezone.make_aware(dt) if timezone.is_naive(dt) else dt
            else:
                app_deadline = None

            event_data = {
                "title": title,
                "description": description,
                "purpose_type": purpose_type,
                "acceptance_mode": acceptance_mode,
                "max_participants": int(max_participants) if max_participants and str(max_participants).isdigit() else None,
                "start_time": start_time,
                "end_time": end_time,
                "application_deadline": app_deadline,
                "venue_name": venue_name,
                "venue_address": venue_address,
                "venue_lat": Decimal(str(venue_lat)) if venue_lat else None,
                "venue_lng": Decimal(str(venue_lng)) if venue_lng else None,
                "geofence_radius_m": int(geofence_radius_m),
                "meals_provided": meals_provided,
                "lodging_provided": lodging_provided,
                "travel_support_provided": travel_support_provided,
                "onboarding_text": onboarding_text,
                "charity_field_ids": charity_field_ids,
            }
            target_owner_id = int(owner_user_id) if owner_user_id and str(owner_user_id).isdigit() else None
            created_event = event_service.admin_create_event(
                request.user,
                data=event_data,
                owner_user_id=target_owner_id,
            )

            if role_title:
                headcount = int(role_headcount) if role_headcount and str(role_headcount).isdigit() else 5
                EventPosts.objects.create(
                    event=created_event,
                    title=role_title,
                    required_headcount=headcount,
                )

            success_message = f"Event '{created_event.title}' (ID: {created_event.id}) successfully created by Super Admin!"
            if request.headers.get("accept") == "application/json":
                return Response(
                    {"status": "created", "event_id": created_event.id, "title": created_event.title},
                    status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
                )
        except Exception as e:
            error_message = str(e)
            if request.headers.get("accept") == "application/json":
                return Response({"error": error_message}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

    charity_fields = list(CharityFields.objects.filter(active=True).order_by("sort_order", "code"))
    approved_coordinators = list(CoordinatorProfiles.objects.select_related("user").filter(status="approved"))
    all_events = list(VEventSummary.objects.all().order_by("-start_at")[:30])

    context = {
        "active_tab": "event_create",
        "charity_fields": charity_fields,
        "approved_coordinators": approved_coordinators,
        "all_events": all_events,
        "is_superuser": bool(request.user.is_superuser),
        "success_message": success_message,
        "error_message": error_message,
    }
    return render(request, "admin_portal/event_create.html", context)


@extend_schema(exclude=True)
@api_view(["GET", "POST"])
@permission_classes([AllowAny])
def events_detail_and_geo_view(request):
    guard = _guard_admin_html(request)
    if guard:
        return guard

    from apps.attendance.models import VGeoAttemptsReview
    from apps.coordination.models import Assignments
    from apps.events.models import EventApplications, Events, VEventPostCapacity, VEventSummary
    from services import moderation_service

    success_message = None
    error_message = None

    if request.method == "POST":
        action = request.POST.get("action") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("action")
        target_event_id = request.POST.get("event_id") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("event_id")
        reason = request.POST.get("reason") or getattr(request, "data", {}).get("reason", "Admin moderation action")

        if not target_event_id:
            error_message = "Event ID is required for moderation action."
        else:
            try:
                target_event_id = int(target_event_id)
                action_type_map = {
                    "cancel": "event_cancel",
                    "event_cancel": "event_cancel",
                    "delete": "event_delete",
                    "event_delete": "event_delete",
                    "restore": "event_restore",
                    "event_restore": "event_restore",
                }
                action_type = action_type_map.get(action)
                if not action_type:
                    raise DomainError(
                        code="invalid_action",
                        message=f"Invalid event moderation action '{action}'.",
                        status_code=400,
                    )

                moderation_service.record_moderation_action(
                    admin_user=request.user,
                    action_type=action_type,
                    event_id=target_event_id,
                    reason=reason,
                )
                success_message = f"Successfully performed '{action_type}' on event #{target_event_id}."
            except Exception as e:
                error_message = str(e)

    # Load event list and optional event inspection
    event_id = request.GET.get("event_id") or request.POST.get("event_id")
    selected_event = None
    selected_posts = []
    selected_applications = []
    selected_assignments = []
    selected_geo_attempts = []

    if event_id and str(event_id).isdigit():
        eid = int(event_id)
        selected_event = Events.objects.filter(id=eid).first()
        if selected_event:
            selected_posts = list(VEventPostCapacity.objects.filter(event_id=eid))
            selected_applications = list(
                EventApplications.objects.select_related("user", "post")
                .filter(event_id=eid)
                .order_by("-applied_at")[:50]
            )
            selected_assignments = list(
                Assignments.objects.select_related("user", "post")
                .filter(event_id=eid)
                .order_by("-created_at")[:50]
            )
            selected_geo_attempts = list(
                VGeoAttemptsReview.objects.filter(event_id=eid).order_by("-attempted_at")[:50]
            )

    events_list = list(VEventSummary.objects.all().order_by("-start_at")[:100])
    geo_attempts = list(VGeoAttemptsReview.objects.all().order_by("-attempted_at")[:100])
    geo_map_json = _build_geo_map_json(selected_event=selected_event)

    context = {
        "active_tab": "events",
        "events_list": events_list,
        "selected_event": selected_event,
        "selected_posts": selected_posts,
        "selected_applications": selected_applications,
        "selected_assignments": selected_assignments,
        "selected_geo_attempts": selected_geo_attempts,
        "geo_attempts": geo_attempts,
        "geo_map_json": geo_map_json,
        "is_superuser": bool(request.user.is_superuser),
        "success_message": success_message,
        "error_message": error_message,
    }
    return render(request, "admin_portal/events_detail.html", context)
